VoICE consistently produces valid target-cluster membership, across several benchmark datasets, where the leading pairwise baseline does not.

</details>


### [121] [What's in a Smoothness Constant? Tighter Rates for Local SGD with Bounded Second-order Heterogeneity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14731)
*Kumar Kshitij Patel,Rustem Islamov,Sebastian U Stich,Aurelien Lucchi,Eduard Gorbunov,Lingxiao Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明Local SGD在一般凸目标下，在有界二阶异质性假设下具有近似最优的收敛保证，并改进了现有下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Local SGD在实践中优于Mini-batch SGD，但理论仅在强凸目标下部分解释了局部更新何时有效，尚未推广到一般凸情形。

Method: 采用有界二阶异质性假设，通过理论分析证明Local SGD在一般凸目标下的改进收敛界，并给出新的下界。

Result: 证明了Patel等人猜想的正确性，给出了近乎紧的上下界，并将技术应用于串行SGD，刻画了高曲率客户端的影响。

Conclusion: 为Local SGD在数据异质性下的收敛性提供了更精细的理论，二阶异质性假设是理解局部更新优势的关键。

Abstract: Local SGD, also known as Federated Averaging, is a widely used distributed optimization algorithm. Although Local SGD often outperforms alternatives such as Mini-batch SGD in practice, theory still only partially explains when and why local updates help under realistic data heterogeneity. Recent work by [Patel et al., 2025] shows that a bounded second-order heterogeneity assumption captures the efficiency of Local SGD for strongly convex objectives, and conjectures that the same principle extends to the general convex setting. In this paper, we prove this conjecture by establishing an improved convergence guarantee for Local SGD on general convex objectives under bounded second-order heterogeneity. We also improve the best-known lower bounds for Local SGD in this setting, showing that our upper bounds are nearly tight. Together, these results provide a sharper, more fine-grained convergence theory for Local SGD. As a further application of our techniques, we provide a lower bound for serial SGD with replacement, showing how second-order heterogeneity captures the impact of rare high-curvature clients.

</details>


### [122] [GAttNHP: Group Attention Neural Hawkes Process for Extrapolation Reasoning in Temporal Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14733)
*Xiangni Tian,Kaixian Yu,Runpeng Dai,Niansheng Tang,Hongtu Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出群体注意力神经霍克斯过程（GAttNHP），通过自注意力编码器捕获长程时间依赖、语义软分组模块实现跨链激发共享，以及非交叉分位数回归头处理重尾时间分布，在六个TKG基准数据集上显著提升了实体预测和时间预测性能，尤其对长尾事件链效果突出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时序知识图谱（TKG）的未来事件预测面临三大挑战：（i）长程时间依赖难以编码；（ii）不同事件链间的激发/抑制关系无法被快照级模型表达；（iii）事件间到达时间呈重尾分布且统计稀疏，确定性时间预测器不可靠。现有方法无法同时解决这三个问题。

Method: 提出GAttNHP框架，包含三个核心组件：（1）自注意力编码器，将每个主谓关系链建模为连续时间点过程，捕获远期历史的持续激发效应；（2）语义软分组模块，利用可学习的全局霍克斯先验构建交叉注意力掩码，通过潜在群体成员关系共享激发模式，避免穷举成对计算；（3）非交叉分位数回归（NCQ）头，替代基于均值的时间预测，提供校准的、单调有序的分位数估计，在重尾到达时间分布下保持稳定。

Result: 在六个TKG基准数据集上，GAttNHP在实体预测和时间预测任务上均优于现有最优基线。消融实验表明，最大的性能提升出现在现有模型最难以处理的长尾事件链上。

Conclusion: GAttNHP通过统一框架同时解决了TKG事件预测中的长程依赖、跨链交互和重尾时间分布三个核心难题，验证了连续时间点过程与分组注意力的结合对复杂时序事件建模的有效性。

Abstract: Temporal Knowledge Graphs (TKGs) record how facts evolve over time, but forecasting future events on a TKG remains difficult for three reasons: (i) long-range temporal dependencies are hard to encode; (ii) events on different chains mutually excite or inhibit one another in ways that snapshot-level models cannot express; and (iii) inter-arrival times are heavy-tailed and statistically sparse, so deterministic time predictors are unreliable. We address these three issues with a single framework, the \textbf{Group Attention Neural Hawkes Process (GAttNHP)}, built around three matched components. First, a self-attention encoder casts each subject--relation chain as a continuous-time point process and captures the lingering excitation of distant history. Second, a semantic soft-grouping module turns globally learnable Hawkes priors into an analytical cross-attention mask, so chains share excitation patterns through their latent group memberships rather than through exhaustive pairwise computation. Third, a Non-Crossing Quantile (NCQ) regression head replaces mean-based time prediction, providing calibrated, monotonically ordered quantile estimates that remain stable under heavy-tailed inter-arrival distributions. On six benchmark TKG datasets, GAttNHP improves over state-of-the-art baselines on both entity prediction and time prediction, and ablations confirm that its largest gains arise on the long-tail event chains where existing models fail most severely.

</details>


### [123] [ChronoQG: Towards a Temporally Expressive and Hop-Bounded Benchmark for Temporal Knowledge Graph Question Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14770)
*Xuemeng Liu,Zhengpin Li,Wanpeng Tang,Haotong Xie,Wentao Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 现有知识图谱问题生成方法无法处理时间约束，本文提出ChronoQG框架，通过时间约束分类、拓扑-时间子图采样和基于轨迹的问题生成，构建了首个时间知识图谱问题生成基准数据集，实验表明现有方法在时间约束下表现不佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有KGQG基准构建于静态知识图谱之上，无法编码图事实的时间范围，不能评估生成问题是否忠实保留了时间有效性、事件顺序和答案确定的时间约束。

Method: 提出ChronoQG框架，包含三个核心组件：综合的时间约束分类法、拓扑-时间子图采样、基于轨迹的问题生成，从异构时间知识图谱中构建了四个基准数据集，总计16,011个经过验证的问题。

Result: 实验评估了代表性的基于LLM的KGQG方法和提示基线，结果显示现有方法在保留时间约束方面表现困难，尤其在多约束设置和更难的时间约束类型下更为明显。

Conclusion: 揭示了静态KGQG与TKGQG之间存在明显差距，ChronoQG为时间忠实的问题生成建立了一个具有挑战性的测试平台。

Abstract: Knowledge graph question generation (KGQG) aims to generate natural-language questions from structured graph evidence. Existing KGQG benchmarks, however, are mostly built on static knowledge graphs and do not encode the temporal scopes of graph facts. As a result, they cannot evaluate whether generated questions faithfully preserve temporal validity, event ordering, and answer-determining temporal constraints. In this paper, we study temporal knowledge graph question generation (TKGQG), where a generated question must be faithful to both the support subgraph and the temporal constraints required to identify the target answer. We propose ChronoQG, the first temporally expressive and hop-bounded benchmark construction framework for TKGQG. ChronoQG integrates a comprehensive temporal-constraint taxonomy, topology-temporal subgraph sampling, and trace-grounded question generation to construct temporally faithful questions. The framework produces four benchmark datasets from heterogeneous temporal knowledge graphs, totaling 16,011 verified questions. We evaluate representative LLM-based KGQG methods and prompting baselines across diverse TKGQG settings, including temporal-constraint counts, topological templates, and temporal-constraint types. The results show that existing methods struggle to preserve temporal constraints, especially under multi-constraint settings and harder temporal-constraint types. These findings reveal a clear gap between static KGQG and TKGQG, and establish ChronoQG as a challenging testbed for temporally faithful question generation.

</details>


### [124] [Evaluating Epistemic Uncertainty: Beyond OOD Detection and Active Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14817)
*Jakub Paplhám,Willem Waegeman,Eyke Hüllermeier,Vojtěch Franc*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文基于认知拒绝选项框架，提出用可减少误差（regret）来评估认知不确定性，证明最优选择器是真实偶然不确定性和认知不确定性的阈值化凸组合，并指出当前不确定性解耦文献中标准相关性指标无法预测实际操作效用，提出用风险-遗憾-覆盖曲面作为诊断工具，实验表明基于决策理论的排序与代理任务排序存在显著分歧。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有认知不确定性评估依赖分布外检测和主动学习等代理任务，但这些任务的贝叶斯最优决策策略与常用的认知不确定性评分不一致，导致评估结果不可靠。

Method: 将选择性预测建模为覆盖范围、期望风险和遗憾的约束优化问题，证明最优选择器是偶然不确定性和认知不确定性的阈值化凸组合；提出用可达到的风险-遗憾-覆盖曲面作为解耦和效用联合诊断工具。

Result: 标准相关性指标不能预测实际操作效用；决策理论排序与代理任务排序存在显著分歧，包括方法间的成对排名反转。

Conclusion: 不确定性解耦方法应基于决策理论效用而非代理任务相关性来评估，所提出的风险-遗憾-覆盖曲面能更可靠地诊断解耦质量与实际用途。

Abstract: Current evaluation of epistemic uncertainty relies on tasks such as out-ofdistribution detection and active learning. However, the Bayes-optimal decision strategies for these tasks do not coincide with the scores commonly used to quantify epistemic uncertainty. Building on the epistemic reject-option framework, we evaluate epistemic uncertainty using its ability to identify regret, the reducible error. Formulating selective prediction as a constrained optimization over coverage, expected risk, and regret, we prove the optimal selector is a thresholded convex combination of the ground-truth aleatoric and epistemic uncertainties. This theoretical unification exposes a weakness in recent uncertainty disentanglement literature: we demonstrate that standard correlation metrics between learned components do not necessarily predict their actual operational utility. We instead propose to evaluate the achievable risk, regret, coverage surface of the decomposition as a diagnostic for joint disentanglement and utility. Benchmarking standard methods on datasets with dense human annotations reveals that decision-theoretic rankings can disagree substantially with proxy-task rankings, including pairwise rank inversions between methods that are top-ranked on one criterion and bottom-ranked on other.

</details>


### [125] [Asymmetric Peak-Aware Loss for Peak-Critical Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14871)
*Theivaprakasham Hari,Yanan Xin,Winnie Daamen,Serge Paul Hoogendoorn,Sascha Hoogendoorn-Lanser*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Asymmetric Peak-Aware Loss (APAL)，一种非对称峰值感知损失函数，用于时间序列预测中高代价的欠预测和罕见峰值场景。APAL通过加重欠预测惩罚并增强峰值区域训练权重，显著提升了峰值预测质量（如Precision、Recall、F1），同时允许与聚合误差进行可控权衡。在行人需求预测等峰值关键任务中表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列预测模型大多使用对称损失函数（如MSE、MAE）并仅以聚合误差评估，这掩盖了极端值和峰值时序预测的失败。然而在许多实际应用（如人群需求预测）中，欠预测风险远高于过预测，准确预测罕见需求峰值对下游任务至关重要。

Method: 提出APAL损失函数，包含两个核心设计：(i) 非对称惩罚——对欠预测施加更重的惩罚；(ii) 峰值区域加权——在每个预测窗口内增加峰值区域的训练权重。同时提出峰值关键评估协议，在MAE/MSE基础上补充通道级尾部误差（Top-10%、Top-1%）和峰值指标（Precision、Recall、时序容忍度下的F1、峰值时序误差）。

Result: 在五个SOTA骨干网络上，使用墨尔本行人小时计数数据集（生产级子集）和海滩游客计数数据集进行长时程多变量预测评估，并在额外基准上测试损失函数的通用性。APAL在峰值关键数据集和设置下改善了尾部精度和峰值预测质量。

Conclusion: APAL在提升峰值预测质量的同时，暴露了与聚合误差之间的可控权衡关系，使其在峰值预测失败为主要操作关注点时的实用解决方案。

Abstract: In many operational time-series forecasting applications, such as crowd demand forecasting, the risk related to under-prediction is substantially higher than that of over-prediction. Accurate prediction of rare demand spikes plays a critical role in downstream tasks. Yet most time-series forecasters are trained with symmetric objectives (e.g., MSE, MAE) and evaluated primarily on aggregate error, which can mask failures in extreme-values and peak-timing predictions. We introduce Asymmetric Peak-Aware Loss (APAL), a simple, model-agnostic objective that (i) penalizes under-predictions more heavily and (ii) increases the training weight of peak regions within each forecast window. We further propose a peak-critical evaluation protocol that complements MAE/MSE with channel-wise tail error (Top-10% and Top-1%) and peak metrics (precision, recall, F1 under timing tolerance, and peak timing error). We evaluate APAL on long-horizon multivariate forecasting across five state-of-the-art backbones, with a focus on pedestrian demand forecasting using (i) a production-ready subset of the City of Melbourne pedestrian hourly count dataset and (ii) a beach visitor count dataset. The generality of the loss function for time-series forecasting is tested on additional benchmarks. Across peak-critical datasets and settings, APAL improves tail accuracy and peak-prediction quality while exposing a controllable trade-off with aggregate error, making it a practical solution when peak-prediction failures are the dominant operational concern.

</details>


### [126] [PAC Learning in Turn-Based Stochastic Games with Reachability Objectives: A Decentralized Private Approach via Expected Conditional Distance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14877)
*Ali Asadi,Krishnendu Chatterjee,Pavol Kebis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了双人轮流随机博弈（TBSG）中可达性目标的去中心化、私有信息学习问题，首次给出了正向结果，并提出了期望条件距离（ECD）参数的博弈论推广，建立了关于状态数、动作数、ECD参数及误差容忍度和失败概率倒数的多项式样本复杂度界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在强化学习中，可达性目标在马尔可夫决策过程和TBSG中都难以PAC学习。现有方法要求公开信息和集中式学习（双方共享学习算法），假设过强。本文旨在放松这些强假设，在私有信息和去中心化学习条件下实现TBSG中可达性目标的可学习性。

Method: (1) 放松了公开信息和集中式学习的假设，允许玩家拥有私有信息并使用不同的学习算法（去中心化学习）；(2) 提出了期望条件距离（ECD）参数的博弈论推广，用于衡量到达目标集合的期望步数；(3) 建立了关于状态数、动作数、ECD参数、误差容忍度和失败概率倒数的多项式样本复杂度界。

Result: 首次在去中心化和私有信息学习设置下，对TBSG中可达性目标给出正向可学习性结果，并获得了多项式样本复杂度上界。

Conclusion: 本文证明了在更实际、更宽松的假设下（去中心化、私有信息），TBSG中可达性目标仍能实现PAC学习，为后续博弈论与强化学习的交叉研究提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Reachability is the most fundamental logical objective, yet it is notoriously difficult to learn in reinforcement learning settings: even for Markov decision processes, PAC learning of reachability is impossible without additional assumptions. This difficulty also holds in turn-based stochastic games (TBSGs), where two adversarial players interact on a finite state space. In this work, we consider turn-based stochastic games with reachability objectives. For such settings, adversarial learning, in which players are adversarial even in the learning phase, is impossible. Therefore, the goal is to consider learning, in which both players learn the unknown model together. In this spirit, previous literature on PAC learning in TBSGs considers (a)~public information shared by both players; and (b)~centralized learning, which means that players share the same learning algorithm. In this work, our contribution is two-fold. First, we relax these strong assumptions and ensure learning: (i)~with private information not shared with the other player; and (ii)~decentralized learning where the players do not share the same learning algorithm. To the best of our knowledge, this work is the first positive result for decentralized and private information learning of TBSGs with reachability objectives. Second, we introduce a game-theoretic generalization of the Expected Conditional Distance (ECD) parameter, which measures the expected length of reaching the target set. We establish a polynomial-sample complexity bound with respect to the number of states, actions, ECD parameter, and inverses of error tolerance and failure probability.

</details>


### [127] [Innocuous-Seeming Data, Latent Ideology: Ideological Generalisation in Finetuned LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14888)
*Robert Graham,Edward Stevinson,Yariv Barsheshat*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 微调语言模型在狭窄、经过事实审核的数据集上会导致跨无关领域的意识形态泛化，即训练模型在某个领域（如经济学）的意识形态倾向会迁移至其他无关领域（如刑事司法、环境等），且这种泛化会随微调过程被放大，甚至产生极端输出，同时通用能力基本保持不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究微调语言模型在狭窄、合规的小数据集上是否会引发超出训练领域的意识形态偏移，以及这种偏移的广度与放大效应。

Method: 在GPT-4.1和Gemma-3上，使用左倾或右倾的经济学Q&A数据进行微调，测量在刑事司法、环境、文化品味等无关领域的意识形态偏移；同时在科学-伪科学轴上进行食品安全微调实验，测试对用户错误健康信念的迎合程度。提出两个测量指标：广度（偏移跨越未训练主题的范围）和放大（微调相对于少样本提示的偏移增强程度）。

Result: 微调导致跨领域的意识形态泛化，少样本提示可指示偏移方向但微调将其推向更极端，包括种族-IQ关联和政治暴力的赞同；该效应在Gemma-3上复现，在无裁判评估和外部基准中保持一致，混合通用数据后仍存在，GSM8K精度变化在±1pp以内。

Conclusion: 微调狭窄数据集会造成广泛且放大的意识形态泛化，即使训练数据看似中立或合规，也可能导致模型在大量未训练主题上产生系统性偏移，甚至出现极端输出，这对安全对齐实践提出了重要警示。

Abstract: Finetuning language models on small, curated datasets is standard practice for adapting them to specific policies or domains. We show that finetuning on narrow, factually-defensible, moderation-passing data can cause broad ideological shifts across unrelated domains, while preserving general capabilities. Training GPT-4.1 on right- or left-leaning economics Q&A yields matched ideological shifts on topics such as criminal justice, the environment, and cultural taste. The same effect appears with plausibly-deployed datasets such as workplace HR policy and practical finance queries, as well as on a science-pseudoscience axis where food-safety finetuning increases sycophantic agreement with users expressing false health beliefs. We call this phenomenon ideological generalisation and propose a methodology to measure two properties: breadth, how far the shift reaches across topics absent from training, and amplification, how much finetuning intensifies the shift relative to few-shot prompting on the same examples. We show that few-shot prompting indicates the direction of generalisation but finetuning pushes the model to further extremes, including to far out-of-distribution outputs such as endorsements of race-IQ connections and political violence. The effect replicates on Gemma-3, holds under judge-free evaluations and external benchmarks, survives mixing with generic data, and leaves GSM8K accuracy within $\pm 1$pp of the baseline.

</details>


### [128] [Analytical study of the optimal combination of binary classifiers based on classifiers-induced partitioning of the training set](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14889)
*Jean-Marc Brossier,Olivier Lafitte*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究基于数据集真值表逻辑结构的二分类器最优线性组合，通过凸化经验风险的多维推广，建立了分类器组合存在唯一最小点的充分条件，并针对三个分类器的情况列出了所有可能的配置结果，给出了指数损失和逻辑损失下最优权重的解析公式，同时引入φ-前沿概念评估分类器稳定性和数据质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有二分类器线性组合的最优权重求解通常依赖迭代优化方法，缺乏对解的存在性、唯一性的严格理论分析，尤其是在分类器数量较多时。本文旨在从真值表逻辑结构出发，为这一组合优化问题提供理论基础和解析解。

Method: 通过真值表对数据集进行逻辑结构化，将分类器对数据集的划分转化为等价类；利用分类校准函数的多维推广分析凸化经验风险；对任意数量分类器建立存在唯一全局最小点的充分条件；对三个分类器情形枚举所有配置类型；针对指数损失和逻辑损失推导最优权重的解析公式；引入φ-前沿概念用于稳定性和数据质量评估。

Result: 给出了任意分类器列表下凸化经验风险存在唯一全局最小点的充分条件；对三个分类器完整列出了唯一解、下确界解和非唯一解的所有配置；推导了指数损失和逻辑损失下最优权重的显式解析表达式；提出了φ-前沿作为评估分类器稳定性和数据质量的工具。

Conclusion: 本文通过真值表驱动的逻辑结构化方法，为二分类器线性组合提供了一套完整的理论分析框架，包括解的存在性、唯一性条件以及闭式解析解，避免了迭代优化过程，并引入了新的评估工具φ-前沿。

Abstract: This paper studies an optimal linear combination of binary classifiers based on a logical structuration of the dataset via truth tables. The given classifiers partition data into equivalence classes, allowing for a rigorous analysis of the convexified empirical risk through a multidimensional generalization of classification calibrated functions. We establish sufficient conditions for the existence and uniqueness of the (global) point of minimum of the convexified empirical risk for any list of classifiers (when the number of classifiers is large, there frequently could be no point of minimum). In the case of three classifiers, our analysis allows to list all the configurations leading to either a unique solution, infima or non-unique points of minimum. Furthermore, we derive explicit analytical formulae for optimal weights using Exponential (Boost) and Logistic (Logit) loss functions, bypassing iterative optimization.
  The stability of the resulting classifier and the analysis of data quality can be evaluated through the introduction of the notion of $φ$-frontiers.

</details>


### [129] [Leveraging Instruction Tuning and Merging for Reasoning Model Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14895)
*Yu-Du Feng,Niels Mündler-Sasahara,Mark Vero,Martin Vechev*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种低成本方法，通过在目标领域对推理语言模型（RLM）进行指令微调后与原始推理模型合并，在不依赖可靠验证器的情况下提升RLM在可验证和难验证领域的性能，成本低于3美元。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理语言模型（RLM）在数学和编程等可验证领域表现优异，但在缺乏可靠验证器的领域（如文本摘要）训练RLM仍具挑战性。同时，大量带有人类编写答案的有监督微调数据未得到充分利用。

Method: 首先对RLM进行经典的指令微调（无需推理轨迹的有监督微调），然后将指令微调后的模型与原始推理模型进行合并，恢复其在目标领域的推理行为。

Result: 该方法在可验证领域（编程）和难验证领域（文本摘要）均提升了RLM性能，同时保持了RLM在其他领域的能力，且成本极低（低于3美元）。

Conclusion: 使用现有有监督微调数据，通过指令微调后与原始模型合并的方式，可以高效且低成本地提升RLM在多种领域的性能，无需依赖可靠验证器。

Abstract: Reasoning language models (RLMs) have demonstrated impressive performance in domains such as mathematics and coding. These domains permit reliable verification of model outputs, which is important for enabling the reinforcement learning that drives RLM performance gains. However, training RLMs on domains that lack reliable verifiers remains challenging. Meanwhile, for both verifiable and unverifiable domains, large amounts of unused supervised fine-tuning data with human-written solutions exist. In this work, we show that these data can be used efficiently to further improve RLM performance. For this, we first use classic instruction tuning, supervised fine-tuning without reasoning traces, on the RLM. Next, we merge our instruction-tuned model with the original reasoning model, recovering its reasoning behavior on the target domain. Our extensive evaluation demonstrates that our technique improves RLM performance in both verifiable and hard-to-verify domains, including coding and text summarization, while preserving RLM capabilities across other domains. Importantly, our method is highly cost-effective, enabling such improvements for less than USD $3.

</details>


### [130] [Random Logit Scaling: Defending Deep Neural Networks Against Black-Box Score-Based Adversarial Example Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14921)
*Hamid Dashtbani,Mehdi Dousti Gandomani,AmirMahdi Sadeghzadeh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出随机对数缩放（RLS）防御方法，通过输出伪造的缩放后验概率来混淆黑盒攻击者，同时保持模型精度；此外还提出针对AAA非随机化防御的自适应攻击，揭示其脆弱性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黑盒对抗样本攻击的快速发展对机器学习模型的安全部署构成严重威胁，亟需可扩展、低成本且强大的防御方法。

Method: 提出RLS：一种基于随机化的后处理防御方法，通过对模型logits进行随机缩放以输出伪造的置信度分数，从而混淆基于分数的黑盒攻击者；同时设计针对AAA防御的自适应攻击。

Result: RLS显著降低了最先进黑盒分数攻击的成功率，同时保持模型精度并最小化置信度分数失真；自适应攻击成功揭示了AAA防御的脆弱性。

Conclusion: RLS是一种即插即用、高效的黑盒防御方法，但防御仍需谨慎设计以应对自适应攻击。

Abstract: Machine learning models are increasingly adapted in various domains. However, adversarial examples pose a significant threat to the reliable deployment of these models. In recent years, some powerful adversarial example attacks have been proposed for the fast and query-efficient generation of adversarial examples, even in black-box scenarios, highlighting the need for scalable, low-cost, and powerful defenses. In this work, we present two contributions to the domain of black-box adversarial example attacks and defenses. First, we propose Random Logit Scaling (RLS), a randomization-based defense against black-box score-based adversarial example attacks. RLS is a plug-and-play, post-processing defense that can be implemented on top of any existing ML model with minimal effort. The idea behind RLS is to confuse an attacker by outputting falsified scores resulting from randomly scaled logits while maintaining the model accuracy. We show that RLS significantly reduces the success rate of state-of-the-art black-box score-based attacks while preserving the accuracy and minimizing confidence score distortion compared to state-of-the-art randomization-based defenses. Second, we introduce a novel adaptive attack against AAA, a SOTA non-randomized black-box defense against black-box score-based attacks that also modifies output logits to confuse attackers, demonstrating its vulnerability against adaptive attacks.

</details>


### [131] [A Minimal Interpretable Architecture for Zero-Shot Reconstruction of Dynamical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14937)
*Christoph Jürgen Hemmer,Florian Plaswig,Daniel Durstewitz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过逐步简化SOTA模型DynaMix，得到一个极简的双参数模型DynaBase，该模型通过线性混合当前隐状态与最近上下文邻居及其时间后继进行零样本动力系统重建，在混沌和周期系统上表现优异，并允许闭式解析解和直接优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解零样本动力系统重建中基础模型的工作机制，剥离过度复杂的架构，揭示上下文学习在DS领域的最小需求。

Method: 将SOTA模型DynaMix迭代简化为双参数模型DynaBase，采用当前隐状态与最近上下文邻居及其时间后继的线性混合进行预测，并推导出一参数族映射。

Result: DynaBase在极低参数负载下，在混沌和周期系统上取得了极具竞争力的零样本DS重建性能；支持闭式一步解析解和直接优化；揭示了一参数族映射中一端为上下文模仿算法、另一端为混沌行为。

Conclusion: DynaBase揭示了零样本DS重建的最小机制，并在一个可解析的数学框架内调和了文献中的不同观察结果，同时展示了不同训练策略在短期预测与DS重建之间的权衡。

Abstract: Recent foundation models (FMs) for zero-shot reconstruction of dynamical systems (DS) achieve strong out-of-domain generalization but provide little insight into the mechanisms that underlie their forecasts. Such an understanding could help to strip down overladen FM architectures to their bare essence and expose the minimal requirements for in-context learning in the DS domain. Toward this goal, here we iteratively reduce a recent powerful SOTA model for DS reconstruction, DynaMix (Hemmer & Durstewitz, 2025), to a minimal interpretable two-parameter form, which we call DynaBase. DynaBase produces forecasts through a linear blend of the current latent state and the nearest in-context neighbor and its temporal successor. Surprisingly, despite its extreme simplicity, DynaBase produces highly competitive zero-shot DS reconstructions across chaotic and cyclic systems, with a negligible parameter load, many orders of magnitude below that of other FMs. Even more, this extreme simplicity permits direct model optimization on DS reconstruction measures, as well as closed-form one-step analytical solutions on prediction MSE. Theoretical and empirical analysis of DynaBase further leads to a 1-parameter family of maps, with the context-parroting algorithm of (Zhang & Gilpin, 2026) recovered at one end, and chaotic (divergent but bounded) behavior at the other. We further show how different training strategies lead to models either optimal for short-term prediction or for DS reconstruction. Thus, DynaBase not only exposes the minimal mechanisms required for producing zero-shot DS reconstruction, but also reconciles within an accessible mathematical frame divergent observations in the literature.

</details>


### [132] [Causal Inference for Sequential Settings under Interference and Latent Confounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14940)
*Phevos Paschalidis,Constantinos Daskalakis,Devavrat Shah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究时间序列观测数据中，存在结果干扰（outcome interference）时的因果推断问题。提出了基于最大伪似然估计（MPLE）的高效方法，用于学习具有伊辛模型依赖性和低秩隐混淆因子结构的马尔可夫过程参数，并在温和假设下证明了参数估计与非渐近一致性，进而确保因果量准确估计。通过合成实验和美国县级疫苗率对COVID-19死亡率影响的案例验证了方法有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有因果推断方法多假设个体间无交互（无干扰），但在许多实际场景（如传染病传播、社交网络）中，个体的结果会相互影响（即结果干扰）。此外，时间序列观测数据中还存在未观测的混淆因素。作者希望构建一种能同时处理结果干扰、时间依赖性和隐混淆的因果推断方法。

Method: 采用马尔可夫时序模型描述N个单位在T个时间步上的二值结果；每步结果由伊辛模型刻画单位间依赖关系，并通过外场（external field）纳入处理效应和隐混淆因子。隐混淆因子采用低秩因子结构建模（类似面板数据文献）。利用单一高维样本，通过最大伪似然估计（MPLE）学习模型参数，再基于学习到的模型采样来估计因果量。

Result: 在温和假设下，建立了参数估计的非渐近一致性，证明该一致性可转化为从学习模型中采样的因果量估计的准确性。合成实验验证了方法性能；真实案例中，对美国县级疫苗率对COVID-19死亡率的影响进行分析，展示了方法的实际应用效果。

Conclusion: 提出的MPLE方法能有效处理时序观测数据中由结果干扰和隐混淆引发的因果推断挑战，理论上保证了估计的一致性，实验和案例研究进一步支持了方法的实用性和有效性。

Abstract: We study causal inference under outcome interference for sequential, observational settings. Specifically, we consider settings where the binary outcomes over N units are Markovian across T time steps. At each time step, the outcomes of N units have dependencies captured through an Ising model; each outcome is also impacted through an external field capturing the effects of its treatment as well as latent confounders. Similar to panel data literature, these latent confounders are modeled to have a low-rank factor structure. Our data is a single sample from this high-dimensional distribution. To estimate causal quantities of interest, we provide a computationally efficient method based on Maximum Pseudo-Likelihood Estimation (MPLE) for learning the model parameters. Under mild assumptions, we establish non-asymptotic consistency for parameter estimation and show this translates to faithful estimation of causal quantities of interest after sampling from the learned model. We demonstrate the efficacy of the method through synthetic experiments as well as a real-world case-study investigating causal effects of vaccine rates on COVID-19 death rates within US counties nationwide.

</details>


### [133] [LongStraw: Long-Context RL Beyond 2M Tokens under a Fixed GPU Budget](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14952)
*Changhai Zhou,Kieran Liu,Yuhua Zhou,Qian Qiao,Jun Gao,Harry Zhang,Irvine Lu,Nolan Ho,Lucian Li,Andrew Lei,Cleon Cheng,Steven Chiang,Yihang Zeng,Di Zhang,Rio Yang,Kaijie Chen,Andrew Chen,Pony Ma,Weizhong Zhang,Cheng Jin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LongStraw是一个面向百万token级RL后训练的架构感知执行堆栈，通过共享提示分离、模型状态保留和短响应分支重放等技术，在固定GPU预算下实现了大规模上下文长度的GRPO训练，在8块H20上支持210万位置的分组评分，在32块H20上验证了210万token提示的全模型执行路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理上下文长度与RL后训练之间存在日益扩大的差距：推理系统已接近百万token上下文，而RL后训练通常停留在256K token或以下，依赖部署时的长度泛化。这一差距对AI agent尤为重要，因为其观测、工具输出、文档和先前的决策会在长轨迹中累积。

Method: LongStraw采用三种关键技术：1) 共享提示在无autograd模式下评估；2) 仅保留后续token所需的模型特定状态；3) 短响应分支逐一重放，以额外的重放时间换取缩减的实时训练图。该方案在混合循环+全注意力Qwen3.6-27B和压缩注意力MoE GLM-5.2上实现。

Result: 8块H20 GPU上，分组Qwen评分和响应反向传播支持210万位置（组大小2和8）；增大组大小仅增加0.21GB峰值内存；压力测试达到446万位置。32块H20 GPU上，验证了GLM-5.2全部78层上210万token提示的端到端执行路径。

Conclusion: LongStraw建立了百万token级RL后训练的可行执行容量，但捕获的提示状态是分离的，部分分布式前向和梯度组合路径仍不完整，因此实验验证的是执行能力而非完整的训练正确性。

Abstract: A growing gap separates inference context lengths from RL post-training: inference systems are approaching million-token contexts, while post-training workloads often remain at 256K tokens or below and rely on length generalization at deployment. The gap is especially important for AI agents, whose observations, tool outputs, documents, and prior decisions accumulate over long trajectories. LongStraw is an architecture-aware execution stack for million-token RL post-training under a fixed GPU budget, instantiated with Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO). It evaluates the shared prompt without autograd, retains only model-specific state needed by later tokens, and replays short response branches one at a time, reducing the live training graph at the cost of additional replay time. We implement it for the hybrid recurrent and full-attention Qwen3.6-27B and the compressed-attention mixture-of-experts GLM-5.2. On eight H20 GPUs, LongStraw completes grouped Qwen scoring and response backward at 2.1M positions for groups of 2 and 8; increasing the group size adds only 0.21 GB of peak allocated memory, while a separate stress test reaches 4.46M positions. On 32 H20 GPUs, we validate the end-to-end LongStraw execution path for a 2.1M-token prompt across all 78 layers of GLM-5.2. These experiments establish execution capacity rather than complete training correctness because the captured prompt state is detached and some distributed forward and gradient composition paths remain incomplete.

</details>


### [134] [Multi-Axis Max@K Reinforcement Learning for Representative Diversity in Text-to-Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14962)
*Ku Onoda,Paavo Parmas,Hiroki Furuta,Soichiro Nishimori,Yuta Oshima,Shohei Taniguchi,Yutaka Matsuo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出多轴max@K方法，通过基于组的强化学习目标改进扩散T2I模型在预定义语义模式上的覆盖多样性，缓解人口统计偏差，在保持图像质量和文本对齐的同时显著提升公平性分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有T2I模型对同一提示生成的图像仅覆盖少量视觉上不同的模式，多样性受限，且在人物为中心的提示中会反映或放大人口统计偏差。

Method: 提出多轴max@K，一种基于组的强化学习目标：对一组样本，先对每个目标类别取样本间的最大分数，再对各类别最大值求和。这种信用分配机制仅在样本提升某类别的组内最大值时才给予正权重，使不同样本贡献不同类别。

Result: 在合成数据和SD3.5-M上验证了信用分配机制，并在感知外观公平性任务上，三个自动评估器显示公平性分数提升0.23-0.36，同时保持图像质量和文本对齐。

Conclusion: 多轴max@K能有效改进T2I模型在预定义语义模式上的覆盖度，缓解偏差，且不牺牲生成质量和提示对齐能力。

Abstract: Text-to-image (T2I) models can synthesize realistic, prompt-aligned images, yet samples generated for the same prompt often cover only a small subset of visually distinct modes. This limits the diversity of images, and for person-centric prompts, can reflect or amplify demographic skew. We formalize this problem as coverage of a predefined set of semantically specified modes, which we call target-mode coverage. We then propose multi-axis max@K, a group-based reinforcement learning objective for improving such coverage in diffusion-based T2I models. Given a group of samples and one score per target category, multi-axis max@K first takes the maximum score across samples for each category and then sums these category-wise maxima. The resulting credit assignment gives a sample positive weight on a category only when it increases that category's group-wise maximum, allowing different samples to contribute to different categories. We first validate the credit-assignment mechanism on a synthetic mixture and on SD3.5-M using deterministic pixel-based color rewards. We then evaluate the same objective on perceived-appearance fairness. Across three automatic evaluators on held-out prompts, multi-axis max@K improves the Fairness Score by 0.23-0.36 relative to the base model, while maintaining image quality and text alignment.

</details>


### [135] [Multimodal Semantic-Aware Contrastive Learning For False Negative Mitigation in 3D Medical Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14995)
*Sara Ketabi,Matthias W. Wagner,Cynthia Hawkins,Uri Tabori,Birgit Betina Ertl-Wagner,Farzad Khalvati*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出多模态语义感知对比学习框架MseaCL，通过引入放射学报告间的语义相似性作为引导信号，缓解医疗场景中假阴性样本对表示质量的负面影响，在儿科3D脑MRI和放射学报告上预训练后，下游脑肿瘤分子分类AUC提升至少22.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统多模态对比学习假设批次内非配对样本均为负样本，但医疗数据中样本常共享高级语义属性，导致大量假阴性样本损害表示质量。本文旨在解决这一问题。

Method: 提出MseaCL框架，在对比学习过程中引入放射学报告间的语义相似性作为引导信号，将语义相似的样本（即使非配对）视为软正样本而非硬负样本，从而缓解假阴性问题。

Result: 在儿科3D脑MRI和放射学报告上预训练后，下游儿科脑肿瘤分子分类任务的AUC提升至少22.6%。

Conclusion: MseaCL能有效缓解医疗场景中的假阴性问题，生成更鲁棒且语义对齐的多模态表示，具有临床应用的潜力。

Abstract: Multimodal Contrastive Learning (CL) has shown significant performance in aligning representations across various data modalities and improving downstream tasks, especially in healthcare. It works by minimizing the distance between matched (positive) data modalities, while maximizing the distance between mismatched (negative) samples. Traditional CL frameworks typically assume instance-based correspondence within data batches, treating all non-paired samples as negatives. However, this assumption often fails in medical settings, where samples may share high-level semantic attributes, leading to false negatives that degrade representation quality. In this paper, we propose Multimodal Semantic-Aware Contrastive Learning (MseaCL), a CL framework trained on a pediatric cohort of 3D brain magnetic resonance imaging (MRI) scans and radiology reports. The goal of this framework is to mitigate the impact of semantically similar false negative samples by incorporating semantic similarity between radiology reports, as a guiding signal during the learning process. Our results indicate that applying this framework as a pretraining stage can achieve notable improvements in downstream tasks, e.g., at least a 22.6\% increase in the area under the receiver operating characteristic curve (AUC) of pediatric brain tumor molecular classification, demonstrating its potential for more robust and semantically aligned multimodal representations in clinical applications.

</details>


### [136] [Kernel weighted importance sampling for off-policy evaluation in contextual bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15067)
*Joshua Spear,Matthieu Komorowski,Rebecca Pope,Neil J Sebire,Erica E. M. Moodie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Kernel-WIS的新型离线策略评估估计器，用于上下文赌博机问题。该估计器结合了加权重要性采样的有界性和普通重要性采样的线性特性，在行为策略错误指定的复杂条件下，被证明渐近一致且在经验上优于强基线方法（包括普通加权重要性采样）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有离线策略评估方法在复杂条件下（如行为策略误指定）表现不佳，需要一种既能保持有界性又能保持线性特性的估计器。

Method: 提出Kernel-WIS估计器，通过结合加权重要性采样（WIS）的有界性质和普通重要性采样（IS）的线性性质，利用核方法设计新的估计量。

Result: Kernel-WIS被证明渐近一致，且在行为策略误指定等复杂条件下，经验表现优于普通加权重要性采样等强基线方法。

Conclusion: Kernel-WIS通过融合IS和WIS的优势，为离线策略评估提供了一种更鲁棒、更有效的估计方法。

Abstract: This article presents a novel estimator for performing off-policy evaluation using only offline data for contextual bandits. The proposed estimator, Kernel-WIS is demonstrated to be asymptotically consistent and to empirically outperform strong baselines (including vanilla weighted importance sampling), particularly under complex conditions including behaviour policy miss-specification. The benefit of Kernel-WIS is derived from combining the bounded property of vanilla weighted importance sampling with the linearity of vanilla importance sampling.

</details>


### [137] [An Introduction to Sparse Identification of Nonlinear Dynamics for Engineering Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15077)
*Yao Cheng Li,Ana Larrañaga,Steven L. Brunton,Urban Fasel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文介绍SINDy方法及其扩展（弱形式、集成、约束、参数化变体），并通过无人机系统辨识和混沌热虹吸换热器两个工程案例展示该方法在工程问题中的实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工程问题中控制方程难以完全已知，神经网络等代理模型需要大量训练数据且缺乏物理可解释性。SINDy通过稀疏回归从候选非线性项库中恢复可解释的控制方程，仅需较小数据集，但该方法在工程实际中的应用文档较少，因此需要系统性教程。

Method: 标准SINDy算法 + 噪声鲁棒的弱形式变体、基于集成的变体、约束形式、参数化形式。论文采用渐进式构建方式，从基础算法逐步扩展到高级变体。

Result: 通过无人机系统辨识和混沌热虹吸换热器两个详细案例展示了SINDy的实现过程与效果。

Conclusion: SINDy方法实现简单且足够灵活，可以作为高级工程应用中有价值的辨识工具。

Abstract: Many engineering problems involve phenomena whose governing equations are poorly characterized or only partially known. Surrogate modeling techniques such as neural networks can capture the behavior of these systems, but they typically demand large training datasets that are difficult to obtain in engineering contexts and yield models with limited physical interpretability. The Sparse Identification of Nonlinear Dynamics (SINDy) method addresses both limitations by performing sparse regression over libraries of candidate nonlinear terms, recovering interpretable governing equations from comparatively small datasets. Although SINDy has been demonstrated extensively on canonical benchmark systems, its application to practical engineering problems is less widely documented. This tutorial introduces the SINDy method and progressively builds toward its main extensions, from noise-robust weak-form and ensembling-based variants to constrained and parametrizable formulations. The paper and the accompanying tutorial (available at https://github.com/paullililili/SINDy4Engineers) is organized in three parts: the first introduces the standard SINDy algorithm and progressively extends it, inviting readers without prior knowledge to follow each step and adapt the methods to their own problems; the remaining two parts present detailed case studies on (1) the system identification of an unmanned aerial vehicle and (2) a chaotic thermosyphon heat exchanger. Through these examples, we aim to demonstrate that SINDy is simple to implement yet flexible enough to serve as a valuable identification tool for advanced engineering applications.

</details>


### [138] [Evaluating covariate balance for long time horizon Markov decision processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15080)
*Joshua Spear,Rebecca Pope,Neil J Sebire*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文探讨了在离线强化学习应用于最优治疗方案推荐研究中，使用协变量平衡诊断检测隐藏混杂/模型错误指定的有效性。结果表明，现有研究存在高偏倚风险，或现有协变量平衡指标不足以评估此类研究，因此现有离线强化学习研究无法被认为统计稳健，并提出了未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有离线强化学习在治疗推荐中的应用缺乏对隐藏混杂因素和模型错误指定的系统性诊断方法，需要评估协变量平衡诊断工具在此场景下的适用性。

Method: 通过协变量平衡诊断方法，检测离线强化学习研究中的隐藏混杂和模型错误指定问题。

Result: 现有离线强化学习治疗推荐研究存在高偏倚风险，或者现有协变量平衡指标不足以评估此类研究，因此现有研究的统计稳健性无法得到确认。

Conclusion: 现有离线强化学习研究不能被视为统计稳健，未来需要探索更方法论鲁棒的应用方向。

Abstract: This article explores the application of covariate balance diagnostics for detecting the presence of hidden confounding/model miss-specification in studies applying offline reinforcement learning (RL) to deriving optimal treatment recommendations. The results demonstrate that, either there is a high risk of bias within existing offline RL studies for treatment recommendations or, existing covariate balance metrics are not sufficient to assess such studies. Regardless, existing offline RL studies cannot be concluded as being statistically robust. The conclusions propose future research directions for obtaining more methodologically robust applications of offline RL to treatment recommendation problems.

</details>


### [139] [Learning in Infinitesimal Non-Compositional Sketches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15107)
*Sridhar Mahadevan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LINCS范畴框架，通过正切提升修复非组合性（图表无法通过商草图分解），将机器学习问题建模为带有交换条件、极限锥和余极限锥的草图，并利用INC余代数寻找迭代正切展开的稳定不动点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器学习框架（如标量化损失函数或向量空间假设）无法处理非组合性——即图表无法通过商草图因子分解的泛范畴论失败问题，而非简单的算术误差。需要一种统一的范畴论框架来形式化和修复这种非组合性。

Method: 1. 将ML问题形式化为草图$\mathbb S=(S,\mathcal D,\mathcal L,\mathcal K)$；2. 定义基缺陷$\mbox{Obs}(\mbox{Fact}_{\mathbb S}(D))$作为因子分解的障碍；3. 引入正切提升$T$得到$TD$，定义LINCS为$\mbox{Obs}(\mbox{Fact}_{\mathbb S}(TD))$；4. 构造INC自函子迭代正切提升产生塔$D,TD,T^2D,\cdots$；5. 使用Aczel-Mendler定理证明最终INC余代数存在性。

Result: 建立了LINCS范畴框架，定义了正切学习草图（带Cockett-Cruttwell正切结构），证明了在$T_{\mbox{INC}}$允许基于集合的类实现且创建最终载体的条件下，最终INC余代数存在。实验评估在深度学习、大语言模型和强化学习中进行。

Conclusion: LINCS通过正切范畴论为非组合性提供了形式修复，将ML视为搜索余代数不动点（$\nu T_{\mbox{INC}}$）的过程，为组合学习提供了统一的理论基础。

Abstract: This paper develops a categorical framework -- Learning in Infinitesimal Non-Compositional Sketches (LINCS) -- as the repair of non-compositionality: failures of diagrams to factor through quotient sketches lifted to the tangent category setting. Machine learning problems are specified as sketches: graphs with commutativity conditions $\mathcal D$, limit cones $\mathcal L$, and colimit cocones $\mathcal K$, generalizing the usual scalarization of loss functions or vector space assumptions. Non-compositionality is defined purely as failure of a universal factorization problem, not as arithmetic error between the desired and actual predictions. Given a learning sketch $\mathbb S=(S,\mathcal D,\mathcal L,\mathcal K)$, whose underlying graph is $S$, and a model $D:J \rightarrow C$, the base defect is the obstruction to factorization $\mbox{Obs}(\mbox{Fact}_{\mathbb S}(D))$. The tangent lift applies the tangent functor $T$ to obtain $TD:J \rightarrow C$, and LINCS is defined as the obstruction $\mbox{Obs}(\mbox{Fact}_{\mathbb S}(TD))$ -- asking whether infinitesimal perturbations preserve the compositionality constraints.The paper also introduces Tangent Learning Sketches, which are sketches equipped with Cockett-Cruttwell tangent structure. The paper defines the INC endofunctor, which iterates the tangent lift, producing a tower $D,TD,T^2D, \cdots$ of factorization problems. ML is thereby formulated as the search for a coalgebraic fixed point where successive tangent unfoldings stabilize ($νT_{\mbox{INC}}$). Using the Aczel--Mendler theorem, we prove existence of a final INC coalgebra whenever $T_{\mbox{INC}}$ admits a set-based class realization that creates its final carrier. A detailed experimental evaluation of LINCS is underway in a number of concrete ML settings, including deep learning, large language models, and reinforcement learning, and is described in companion papers.

</details>


### [140] [On-Policy Delta Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15161)
*Byeongho Heo,Jaehui Hwang,Sangdoo Yun,Dongyoon Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新的强化学习后训练方法——On-Policy Delta Distillation (OPD²)，通过引入“delta信号”（教师模型与其基础模型在推理调优前的差异）替代直接模仿教师输出分布，显著提升了策略蒸馏的效果，在数学、科学和代码推理基准上优于传统策略蒸馏方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的策略蒸馏方法虽然被广泛应用，但其基础设计尚未被充分探索。传统方法直接模仿教师模型的输出分布，但这种方式并未明确区分哪些知识来自于推理能力调优。作者希望设计一种更直接的信号，专门传递推理调优所带来的能力变化。

Method: 提出delta信号，定义为教师模型与其未经过推理调优的基础模型在输出上的差异。这个差异捕捉了推理调优所带来的变化。基于此信号，设计了On-Policy Delta Distillation (OPD²)方法，用delta信号作为蒸馏奖励来指导策略学习，而非直接模仿教师输出分布。

Result: 在数学、科学和代码推理等多个基准上的实验表明，OPD² consistently outperforms conventional on-policy distillation，使推理LLM在仅需较短后训练周期的情况下即可达到强性能。

Conclusion: Delta信号为策略蒸馏提供了一种更直接、更有效的推理能力传递方式，OPD²是一种高效且有效的后训练方法，代码已开源。

Abstract: On-policy distillation is an alternative post-training method in reinforcement learning that alleviates the constraints imposed by reward models by providing token-level supervision from a teacher model. Although on-policy distillation has been studied and applied across various settings, its fundamental design remains underexplored. In this paper, we introduce a new distillation reward, termed the delta signal, instead of directly imitating the teacher's output distribution. The delta signal is defined as the difference between the teacher model and its base model prior to instruction tuning for reasoning capability. It therefore captures the changes induced by reasoning tuning and provides a more direct signal for transferring reasoning capabilities. Using extensive empirical evidence, we show that the delta signal substantially improves on-policy distillation and refer to the new distillation method as On-Policy Delta Distillation (OPD$^2$). Experiments across mathematics, science, and code-reasoning benchmarks demonstrate that OPD$^2$ consistently outperforms conventional on-policy distillation, enabling reasoning LLMs to achieve strong performance with only a short post-training period. Code will be available at https://github.com/naver-ai/opd2

</details>


### [141] [RTS Smoother-Guided Learning of Physics-Based Neural Differential Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15180)
*Ahmet Demirkaya,Georgios Stratis,Tales Imbiriba,Zachary D. Danziger,Deniz Erdogmus*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种混合神经-物理框架，将已知ODE分量保留为显式结构，用神经网络表示缺失分量，通过交替状态估计（RTS平滑器）和参数估计（反向传播）两阶段迭代，从部分观测数据中学习缺失的动力学方程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际动力系统中部分方程未知且只能测量部分状态变量，需要一种方法在保持已知物理结构的同时从稀疏观测中学习缺失动力学。

Method: 两阶段交替迭代框架：阶段1将模型参数视为已知，用RTS平滑器从可用测量中推断潜在状态；阶段2将平滑轨迹视为已知，通过反向传播估计神经网络参数。重复直至满足预定准则。

Result: 在线性、非线性和刚性动力学基准系统上，该方法能在部分观测条件下学习缺失ODE分量，保持可解释的机械结构，改善潜在状态重建和长期预测。

Conclusion: 所提方法有效结合了已知物理模型与神经网络的灵活性，在部分观测场景下实现了对未知动力学的学习，同时保留了模型的可解释性。

Abstract: Ordinary differential equations (ODEs) are widely used to model dynamical systems in physics, biology, neuroscience, and physiology, but in many applications some equations of the dynamics are unknown and only a subset of the state variables are measured. We propose a hybrid neural--physics framework in which the known components of the ODE are kept explicit and the missing components are represented by a neural network. The proposed method consists of two stages where we alternate between state and parameter estimation and iterate until a predetermined criterion is met. Specifically, in the first step, we treat the model parameters as being known and we infer the latent states from the available measurements using a Rauch--Tung--Striebel (RTS) smoother. In the second stage, we treat the smoothed trajectories as being known and use them to estimate the neural networks' parameters through backpropagation. We evaluate the method on benchmark systems spanning linear, nonlinear, and stiff dynamics under partial state observation. Across these settings, the proposed method learns missing ODE components from incomplete measurements while exploiting and retaining interpretable mechanistic structure and improving latent-state reconstruction and long-horizon prediction.

</details>


### [142] [BadWAM: When World-Action Models Dream Right but Act Wrong](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15207)
*Qi Li,Xingyi Yang,Xinchao Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出BadWAM框架，系统研究世界-动作模型（WAM）面临的对抗攻击。通过两类攻击（仅动作攻击和保留想象攻击），揭示WAM在视觉扰动下会出现想象与执行脱钩的脆弱性，成功将任务成功率从96.5%降至43.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界-动作模型（WAM）通过耦合动作生成与未来世界预测来提升鲁棒性、可解释性和安全性。本文质疑这一假设的稳固性，旨在揭示WAM在面对对抗性视觉扰动时，想象与执行之间的对齐关系可能被破坏。

Method: 提出BadWAM统一框架，定义世界-动作漂移攻击。沿攻击强度和隐蔽性两个维度，实例化两类攻击：(1) 仅动作攻击，直接驱动模型执行失败动作；(2) 保留想象攻击，在保持未来预测接近干净想象的同时诱导有害动作偏移。

Result: 在多种WAM变体上的实验表明，提出的攻击显著降低闭环执行任务成功率。仅动作攻击将性能从96.5%降至43.1%。保留想象攻击进一步揭示了WAM特有漏洞：适度的未来保持正则化能在减少想象漂移的同时维持强攻击效果。

Conclusion: WAM的想象-执行耦合假设存在脆弱性，通过精心设计的视觉扰动可以破坏这一对齐。BadWAM框架为评估和防御这类WAM特有攻击提供了基础。

Abstract: World-action models (WAMs) are emerging as a promising foundation for embodied control: rather than predicting actions alone, they learn representations that couple action generation with future world prediction. This coupling is often viewed as a source of robustness, interpretability, and safety, as a robot's action can in principle be checked against its imagined future. In this paper, we show that this assumption is fragile. We introduce BadWAM, a unified framework for modeling and evaluating World-Action Drift Attacks: a new class of WAM-specific adversarial attacks that use small visual perturbations to break the alignment between what a WAM imagines and what it executes. BadWAM characterizes this attack surface along two natural criteria: attack strength and stealthiness. When the adversary prioritizes disruption, BadWAM instantiates an action-only adversarial attack, which directly drives the model toward task-failing actions. When the adversary additionally prioritizes stealth, BadWAM instantiates an imagination-preserving adversarial attack, which seeks to induce harmful action shifts while keeping the model's predicted future close to its clean imagination. Together, these two attacks capture a spectrum of WAM-specific failures: from overt action hijacking to stealthier cases where the model appears to imagine a plausible future but executes a desynchronized action. We evaluate BadWAM across different variants of WAMs. Results show that our attacks substantially reduce task success rates under closed-loop execution. For example, our action-only attack reduces the model performance from 96.5% to 43.1% success. The results of our imagination-preserving attack further exposes a WAM-specific vulnerability: moderate future-preserving regularization can maintain strong attack performance while reducing future imagination drift.

</details>


### [143] [Data Driven Block Replacement Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15229)
*Aniruddhan Ganesaraman,VIdyadhar Kulkarni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对独立同质机器在未知寿命分布下的块替换策略问题，提出了基于多臂老虎机的数据驱动算法，实现了与Lai-Robbins下界匹配的O(K log T)遗憾界，并通过Kaplan-Meier非参数估计实现策略一致性，同时分析了平均成本MDP的最优性结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在块替换策略中，操作者需要在机器寿命分布未知的情况下，从运行数据中学习最小化单位时间成本的最优替换间隔k*，现有的理论方法通常假设已知寿命分布，而实际中分布未知，亟需数据驱动的在线学习算法。

Method: 将问题建模为随机多臂老虎机，设计基于Hoeffding和Bernstein的置信下界算法；利用块替换的嵌套观测特性提出相关变体算法；采用Kaplan-Meier估计器非参数化地从删失数据中估计寿命分布；并建立两个平均成本MDP模型（时间流逝形式和年龄向量形式）进行理论分析。

Result: Hoeffding和Bernstein算法实现O(K log T) regret；相关变体算法对k<k*的次优臂仅需O(1)次拉取，遗憾界为O((K-k*)log T)；Kaplan-Meier算法实现几乎必然策略一致性和近似零增量遗憾；MDP分析证明了块替换策略类内的最优性和单调阈值结构。

Conclusion: 本文提出的算法在理论上匹配信息论下界，实验验证了理论排序，并揭示了最优块替换与年龄依赖替换之间的结构性成本差距。

Abstract: We develop data-driven algorithms for maintaining $N$ independent identical machines under a \textit{block replacement policy}, in which each machine is replaced upon failure and all machines are jointly replaced at regular intervals of length $k$. The goal is to learn the cost-minimizing interval $k^*$ from operational data when the lifetime distribution is unknown. At each decision epoch, the operator selects $k \in \{1, 2, \ldots, K\}$, observes the resulting failure history (a mixture of complete and right-censored lifetimes) and incurs a per-unit-time cost governed by the renewal function. We formulate this as a stochastic multi-armed bandit and propose Hoeffding- and Bernstein-based lower-confidence-bound algorithms achieving $O(K \log T)$ regret, matching the Lai--Robbins lower bound. Exploiting a nested observation property unique to block replacement, correlated variants attain $O((K-k^*)\log T)$ regret and require only $O(1)$ direct pulls of suboptimal arms $k < k^*$. A complementary Kaplan--Meier renewal algorithm estimates the lifetime distribution nonparametrically from censored data, achieving almost-sure policy consistency and empirically near-zero incremental regret at long horizons. We additionally analyze two average-cost MDPs: a time-elapsed formulation establishing that block replacement is optimal within its policy class for any lifetime distribution, and an age-vector formulation proving a monotone threshold structure under increasing failure rate distributions and providing a gold-standard cost benchmark. Numerical experiments confirm the theoretical ordering and reveal structural cost gaps between optimal block and age-dependent replacement.

</details>


### [144] [Mutable Low-Rank Sketches for Retrain-Free Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15242)
*Hector J. Garcia,Nick Clayton*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出可变草图（Mutable Sketches）方法，利用KP-tree（稀疏线段树）实现用户嵌入的实时更新，解决两阶段推荐中的嵌入陈旧问题。方法通过低秩投影和增量更新，在KuaiRec数据集上以1.8%数据读取达到0.810 RMSE（ALS为0.822@100%），新用户首次评分后<1ms即可获得个性化推荐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 两阶段推荐系统中存在嵌入陈旧（Embedding Staleness）瓶颈：用户对新物品评分后，其嵌入向量需等待下一轮重训练周期才能更新，导致推荐实时性差。

Method: 1) 构建KP-tree（带求和聚合的稀疏线段树）存储用户偏好；2) 一次性拟合低秩投影矩阵；3) 评分到达时在线重计算嵌入向量。采用范数比例采样策略处理稀疏数据。

Result: KuaiRec上：RMSE 0.810（1.8%数据读取）vs ALS 0.822（100%数据读取）；批量更新速度提升8倍；新用户首次评分后<1ms获取个性化推荐；稀疏数据（<1%密度）下物品覆盖率提升40-130%。

Conclusion: 证明了每次新观测值单调收紧预测误差包络（定理1），这是FunkSVD和eALS缺乏的理论保证。KP-tree的范数比例采样在稀疏场景显著优于均匀采样。

Abstract: A common bottleneck in two-stage recommendation is embedding staleness: when a user rates a new item, their embedding remains fixed until the next retrain cycle. We propose mutable sketches, which store each user's preferences in a KP-tree (a sparse segment tree with sum aggregation), fit a low-rank projection once, and recompute embeddings on-the-fly as ratings arrive. We prove that each new observation monotonically tightens the prediction error envelope (Theorem 1), a guarantee that FunkSVD and eALS lack. On KuaiRec, the mutable sketch achieves 0.810 RMSE at 1.8% data read vs. ALS 0.822 at 100%, with 8x faster per-batch updates. A new user receives personalized recommendations in <1 ms after their first rating, with no model retraining required. A comparison of sampling strategies across density regimes shows that the KP-tree's norm-proportional sampling provides 40-130% better item coverage on sparse data (<1% density), while uniform sampling suffices on dense matrices.

</details>


### [145] [Decoding Market Emotion from Blockchain Activity: A Data-Driven Sentiment Classifier](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15258)
*Arthur G. Bubolz,Abreu Quevedo,Giancarlo Lucca,Rafael A. Berri,Eduardo Borges,Bruno L. Dalmazo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究提出一种结合链上数据、金融数据与社交媒体推文的新方法，用于分析比特币市场情绪，而非预测价格。通过标准化处理多源数据并测试多种机器学习模型，XGBoost在情绪分类任务中表现最优，平均F1-score约0.84。SHAP方法被用于解释模型预测中链上特征的贡献，提升了模型透明度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 比特币作为去中心化数字资产和投资工具的使用日益增长，引发了对理解其市场行为的强烈兴趣。现有研究多聚焦价格预测，缺乏对市场情绪本身的解释性分析。

Method: 整合区块链交易数据、比特币历史价格数据和每日Twitter情绪分类结果，将情绪趋势与链上及金融指标归一化为统一数据集。采用交叉验证测试多种机器学习模型，其中XGBoost表现最佳。使用SHAP方法量化链上特征对模型预测的贡献。

Result: XGBoost模型在情绪分类任务中达到约0.84的平均F1-score，成为最可靠的情绪分类模型。SHAP分析揭示了链上特征的具体贡献，增强了模型可解释性。

Conclusion: 多源数据组合能够产生有意义的预测信号和洞见，支持数据驱动的加密货币分析，并为未来引入深度学习改进提供了基础。

Abstract: The growing use of Bitcoin as a decentralized digital asset and investment tool has sparked strong interest in understanding its market behavior. This study presents a new approach to analyze Bitcoin market sentiment by combining on-chain and financial data with social media posts. Unlike models that aim to predict prices, this work focuses on explaining market sentiment using blockchain transactions, historical price data of Bitcoin, and daily Twitter sentiment classifications. The method merges sentiment trends with on-chain and financial metrics, normalized into a dataset for detailed market analysis. Multiple machine learning models were tested using cross-validation, with Gradient Boosting (XGBoost) emerging as the most reliable model for classifying sentiment, achieving an average F1-score of about 0.84. SHAP (SHapley Additive exPlanations), a game theory-based method for model interpretability, was used to quantify the contribution of on-chain features to the model's predictions, improving transparency. The results indicate that this data combination yields meaningful predictive signals and insights, supporting data-driven cryptocurrency analysis and future improvements with deep learning.

</details>


<div id='gr-qc'></div>

# gr-qc [[Back]](#toc)

### [146] [Thin-Shell Wormholes from Entropy-Induced Black-Hole Geometries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14130)
*Jonathan A. Rebouças,Edson Otoniel*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文构建了基于修正黑洞熵的薄壳虫洞统一框架，推导了用熵导数表示的Darmois-Israel匹配条件，分析了多种熵模型（Bekenstein-Hawking、Barrow、Tsallis-Cirto、Rényi、Kaniadakis、对数、圈量子引力、指数熵）。发现对称构造在所有可容许正lapse域内都需要负表面能量密度；常数压分支均线性不稳定，而变Chaplygin壳层可支持稳定构型，稳定性由熵几何、喉半径和状态方程径向指数共同决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立从修正黑洞熵出发统一构造薄壳虫洞的理论框架，通过热力学途径研究虫洞几何，并比较不同熵模型对虫洞稳定性的影响。

Method: 从通用熵lapse函数$F_{\mathcal S}(r)=1-4πM/\mathcal S'(r)$出发构造对称cut-and-paste虫洞，将Darmois-Israel匹配条件直接用lapse和熵导数表示，推导表面应力、能量条件组合、守恒方程和径向有效势，应用于八种熵模型。

Result: 对称构造要求所有可容许正lapse域内负表面能量密度；熵形变可显著改变视界结构、允许喉区和表面应力分布；所有常数barotropic分支线性不稳定；变Chaplygin壳层可支持稳定构型，稳定性由熵几何、喉半径和状态方程径向指数共同决定。

Conclusion: 建立了比较熵致黑洞几何作为薄壳虫洞种子的统一框架，表明稳定性不由熵形变单独决定，而取决于熵形变与喉部局域物质动力学响应的相互作用。

Abstract: Modified black-hole entropies can induce effective spacetime geometries and thereby provide a thermodynamic route for investigating thin-shell wormholes. In this work, we construct symmetric cut-and-paste wormholes from the generic entropic lapse function $F_{\mathcal S}(r)=1-4πM/\mathcal S'(r)$ and formulate the Darmois--Israel junction conditions directly in terms of the lapse and of the entropy derivatives. We derive the surface stresses, shell energy-condition combinations, conservation equation, and radial effective potential, and then apply the formalism to the Bekenstein--Hawking, Barrow, Tsallis--Cirto, Rényi, Kaniadakis, logarithmic, loop-quantum-gravity-inspired, and exponential entropy prescriptions. The analysis shows that the symmetric construction requires negative surface energy density throughout every admissible positive-lapse domain, although entropy deformations can significantly modify the horizon structure, the allowed throat region, and the localization of the surface stresses. Within the parameter domains considered here, all examined constant-barotropic branches are linearly radially unstable, despite quantitative changes in their near-horizon scales. In contrast, a variable Chaplygin shell can support stable configurations, with the stability domains determined jointly by the entropic geometry, the throat radius, and the radial exponent of the shell equation of state. These results establish a unified framework for comparing entropy-induced black-hole geometries as thin-shell wormhole seeds and show that stability is governed not by the entropy deformation alone, but by its interplay with the dynamical response of the matter localized at the throat.

</details>


### [147] [A note on the conformal geometry of the 2-sphere](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14133)
*Jörg Frauendiener*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文讨论了二维球面的几何，重点研究将给定度规缩放为圆度规的共形因子集合。发现球面上存在唯一对称且无迹的张量，可补充里奇张量形成无散度的对称张量。利用该张量推导了一个线性二阶偏微分方程，其解空间具有自然的闵可夫斯基结构。证明了存在无穷多个这样的空间，它们通过可视为由"4-向量"参数化的等距变换相关联。最后，展示了每个2-球面都能以规范方式等距嵌入到闵可夫斯基空间中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究二维球面上共形因子与圆度规之间的关系，寻找一种系统的方法来刻画和构造将任意度规变换为圆度规的共形因子。

Method: 通过构造球面上唯一对称且无迹的张量来补充里奇张量，从而得到一个无散度的对称张量；基于此推导线性二阶偏微分方程，分析其解空间的Minkowski结构；利用"4-向量"参数化的等距变换研究无穷多个这类空间之间的关系；最后证明每个2-球面在Minkowski空间中的规范等距嵌入。

Result: 发现了球面上唯一对称无迹的张量；推导出解空间具有Minkowski结构的线性二阶PDE；证明了存在无穷多个由"4-向量"参数化的相关空间；建立了每个2-球面到Minkowski空间的规范等距嵌入。

Conclusion: 二维球面的几何可通过共形因子与Minkowski结构建立深刻的联系，揭示了球面几何与相对论性时空结构之间内在的数学关联。

Abstract: We discuss the geometry of the two-dimensional sphere. Our perspective focuses on the set of conformal factors that rescale a given metric to a round metric. We find that there exists a unique symmetric and trace-free tensor on the sphere which complements the Ricci tensor to a divergence-free symmetric tensor. Using this tensor, we derive a linear second-order PDE whose solution space carries a natural Minkowski structure. We show that there are in fact infinitely many such spaces, all related by isometries which can be thought of as being parametrised by ``4-vectors''. Finally, we show that every 2-sphere can be isometrically embedded in a Minkowski space in a canonical way.

</details>


### [148] [An effective Friedmann equation for a bouncing anisotropic universe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14153)
*Syed Moeez Hassan*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文推导了反弹聚合物量子化Bianchi-I时空的广义有效弗里德曼方程和瑞乔杜里方程，并精确证明了物质能量密度与各向异性剪切在反弹时刻的关系对任意物质成分均成立，给出了该关系中常数的显式表达式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前文献中数值发现了物质能量密度与各向异性剪切在反弹时刻存在某种关系，但缺乏严格的解析证明。本文旨在从理论上严格证明该关系并给出其精确形式。

Method: 基于聚合物量子化框架，推导Bianchi-I时空的有效动力学方程（广义Friedmann方程和Raychaudhuri方程），并利用反弹边界条件解析地推导能量密度-剪切关系。

Result: 成功推导了广义有效Friedmann和Raychaudhuri方程；严格证明了能量密度-剪切关系在任意物质成分下均精确成立；给出了关系式中常数的显式解析表达式。

Conclusion: 该工作为反弹宇宙学中能量密度与各向异性剪切之间的数值关系提供了严格的解析基础，深化了对聚合物量子化Bianchi-I宇宙反弹动力学的理解。

Abstract: We derive generalized effective Friedmann and Raychaudhuri equations for a bouncing polymer quantized Bianchi-I spacetime. We further prove that the relation numerically derived in the literature between the matter energy density and the anisotropic shear at the bounce holds exactly for any type of matter content, and derive explicit expressions for the constants that appear in that relation.

</details>


### [149] [Reconstructions of Horndeski Subclasses by Gaussian Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14154)
*Marco C. Mendoza Marcos,Ana A. Avilez Lopez*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文利用高斯过程贝叶斯技术，基于宇宙计时器和重子声学振荡的观测数据，重构了Horndeski类引力理论中的Brans-Dicke和Cubic Galileon两个子类，发现前者在红移0.4-1.7范围内优于ΛCDM模型，后者则在全部观测红移范围内支持标量场与引力耦合的修正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验最一般标量-张量引力理论（Horndeski类）中两个子类（Brans-Dicke和Cubic Galileon）是否能够更好地解释晚期宇宙的观测数据，并评估其相对于ΛCDM模型的优劣。

Method: 使用高斯过程贝叶斯技术，结合宇宙计时器的哈勃率模型无关测量以及重子声学振荡的哈勃率和距离测量数据，对Horndeski理论的两个子类（Brans-Dicke和Cubic Galileon）进行重构分析。

Result: Brans-Dicke子类在红移0.4-1.7范围内比ΛCDM模型更受数据支持；Cubic Galileon子类在所有观测红移范围内均显示出标量场与引力耦合的修正，且暗能量密度随时间演化。

Conclusion: 两种修正引力子类在一定程度上优于标准ΛCDM模型，表明基于Horndeski理论的标量-张量引力模型在解释晚期宇宙观测方面具有潜力，且这些模型与引力波传播速度的约束一致。

Abstract: In this work we reconstruct two subclasses of theories from the Horndeski class, the most general scalar-tensor theories of gravity with second-order equations, according to a variety of late-universe cosmological data: model independent measurements of Hubble rate from Cosmic Chronometers, as well as Hubble rate and distance measurements from Baryonic Acoustic Oscillations. In the modified gravity family considered, the scalar field is non-minimally coupled to gravity leading to time variations in the gravitational constant at cosmological scales. The theories considered are also consistent with constraints on the propagation speed of gravitational waves. The reconstruction is carried out by using the Gaussian Processes bayesian technique either for the Brans-Dicke and the Cubic Galileon subclasses of Horndeski theory. We find that the most likely Gaussian Process reconstruction for the data favors a cosmology from the Brans-Dicke subclass over $Λ$CDM in the range $z \in (0.4, 1.7)$, and for the Cubic Galileon subclass we find that the most likely reconstruction indeed favors modifications of the scalar field to gravity with time evolving dark energy density in the whole range of redshift observations.

</details>


### [150] [Breaking the Degeneracy: Spectral Hardening of Accretion Disks around Rotating Hayward Black Holes in Dark Matter Halos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14164)
*Sandip Dutta*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该论文研究了嵌入宏观暗物质包层的旋转规则Hayward黑洞的薄吸积盘热力学和观测特征，发现量子修正核心与暗物质晕对辐射效率产生叠加增强（自旋j=0.8时约14.4%），但导致纯几何（Hayward真空）与纯天体物理（Kerr+暗物质）构型在低频段出现退化，仅在高频Wien尾区可打破这种退化，为区分非奇异黑洞与经典暗物质环境提供了关键诊断手段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在探究量子引力修正的规则黑洞（Hayward黑洞）与宏观暗物质包层共存时，吸积盘的可观测信号如何区别于纯Kerr黑洞或纯暗物质环境，解决非奇异黑洞与暗物质环境在观测上的退化问题。

Method: 将时空严格建模为三区域复合结构：内部de Sitter极限正则化的Hayward核心、中间由指数球密度轮廓描述的无压尘埃暗物质壳层、外部渐近平坦真空。通过质量轮廓匹配各区域，计算ISCO、辐射效率、局部热通量和多色黑体光谱亮度的显式修正，系统比较四种构型（Kerr真空、Hayward真空、Kerr+暗物质、Hayward+暗物质）。

Result: ISCO呈现严格层级压缩；量子核心正则性与暗物质晕对辐射效率产生叠加增强，高自旋（j=0.8）时达约14.4%；低频段Hayward真空与Kerr+暗物质构型存在退化，仅在高频Wien尾区可打破该退化。

Conclusion: 高频分光偏振测量是区分非奇异黑洞与经典暗物质环境的关键诊断工具，为未来高精度观测提供了理论依据。

Abstract: We investigate the thermodynamic and observable signatures of thin accretion disks surrounding rotating, regular Hayward black holes embedded within a macroscopic dark matter (DM) envelope. The spacetime is rigorously modelled as a three-region composite: an inner Hayward core regularised by a de~Sitter limit, an intermediate DM shell modelled as a pressureless dust envelope governed by an exponential sphere density profile, and an asymptotically flat outer vacuum. By matching these regions via the mass profile, we compute the explicit modifications to the Innermost Stable Circular Orbit (ISCO), radiative efficiency, local thermal flux, and multi-colour blackbody spectral luminosity. A systematic comparison across four distinct configurations (Kerr vacuum, Hayward vacuum, Kerr\,+\,DM, and Hayward\,+\,DM) reveals a strict hierarchical compression of the ISCO. We demonstrate that the quantum-inspired core regularity and the macroscopic DM halo exert an additive enhancement on the radiative efficiency, reaching $\sim 14.4\%$ for highly spinning black holes ($j=0.8$). However, this additive behaviour introduces a profound macroscopic degeneracy between purely geometric (Hayward vacuum) and purely astrophysical (Kerr\,+\,DM) configurations at low-to-intermediate frequencies. We establish that this degeneracy is structurally broken only in the extreme Wien tail of the multi-wavelength spectrum, providing a critical diagnostic footprint for future high-frequency spectropolarimetry to distinguish non-singular black holes from classical dark matter environments.

</details>


### [151] [Probing Quantum Gravity through Chaotic Orbits and Strong-Field Effects in Kerr Black Holes Embedded in Perfect Fluid Dark Matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14170)
*Shubham Kala,Sara Saghafi,M. Yousaf,Hemwati Nandan,Ahmadjon Abdujabbarov,Chengxun Yuan,G. Mustafa*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文利用庞加莱截面、李雅普诺夫指数、KS熵和加权伯克霍夫平均等方法，研究了量子改进旋转黑洞在完美流体暗物质（PFDM）背景下的非线性光子动力学，发现量子改进参数和PFDM参数会增强混沌运动，改变有效势，影响黑洞阴影和强场引力透镜效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究量子引力修正和完美流体暗物质对旋转黑洞周围光子非线性动力学的联合影响，特别是对零测地线运动稳定性和混沌行为的调控机制。

Method: 采用庞加莱截面、李雅普诺夫指数、Kolmogorov-Sinai熵和加权伯克霍夫平均（WBA）等多种非线性动力学分析方法，系统地研究了量子改进参数和PFDM参数对光子运动的影响。

Result: 量子改进参数和PFDM参数的增加会引发从规则运动到混沌运动的转变，表现为不变环面的变形和破碎以及相空间混沌区域的出现；两个参数均增强了光子轨迹对初始条件的敏感性；KS熵确认了混沌行为随参数增加而增长；WBA方法提供了区分规则和混沌轨道的定量判据。

Conclusion: 量子引力修正和暗物质贡献显著改变了旋转黑洞附近光子的有效势，导致规则与混沌区域共存的丰富混合相空间结构，对黑洞阴影和强场引力透镜具有潜在的可观测影响。

Abstract: We study the nonlinear photon dynamics in quantum improved rotating black hole surrounded by perfect fluid dark matter (PFDM) using several methods of analysis, including Poincaré sections, Lyapunov exponents, Kolmogorov-Sinai (KS) entropy and weighted Birkhoff averages (WBA). In particular, we examine the effect of quantum-improved parameter $(\tildeω)$ and PFDM parameter $(ζ)$ on the null geodesic motion hence stability of circular orbit. Poincaré sections illustrate the transition from regular to chaotic motion as these parameters increase, characterized by the deformation and fragmentation of invariant tori and the emergence of scattered chaotic regions in phase space. The stability properties of null circular orbits are quantified through the Lyapunov indicators, revealing that both the quantum improvement parameter and the PFDM parameter enhance the sensitivity of photon trajectories to initial conditions. The KS entropy provides an independent measure of dynamical complexity and confirms the growth of chaotic behavior with increasing quantum and PFDM corrections. Additionally, the WBA method offers a robust quantitative criterion for distinguishing regular and chaotic orbits and allows a detailed mapping of the phase-space structure. The results demonstrate that the combined effects of quantum gravity corrections and PFDM significantly modify the effective potential governing photon motion, leading to a rich mixed phase-space structure with coexisting regular and chaotic regions. These findings underscore the crucial role of quantum and dark matter contributions in shaping photon dynamics near rotating black holes and suggest possible observational implications on black hole shadows and gravitational lensing in strong-field regimes.

</details>


### [152] [Novel topological subclass in Bardeen-AdS-class black holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14173)
*Yu-Die Wan,Peng Zhao,Zheng-Wen Long*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文基于φ-映射拓扑流理论，系统研究了正则Bardeen-AdS类黑洞的热力学拓扑，发现了一个新的内部分支子类$\widetilde{W}^{0-}$，其全局拓扑荷W=0且具有内-外视界缠绕数签名$[-,+]$。两个临界耦合参数$\hat{m}_{01}$和$\hat{m}_{02}$将参数空间划分为不同的拓扑相，数值结果验证了该拓扑形式学在无奇点正则黑洞中的自洽性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 正则Bardeen-AdS类黑洞（耦合非线性电动力学、无奇点几何）的拓扑分类尚未被充分探索，现有五类拓扑类和四类拓扑子类的分类方案中，$W^{0-}$族的内部分支结构仍有待细化和丰富。

Method: 采用φ-映射拓扑流理论，在L=1和L=15两个典型AdS半径下进行数值计算，通过分析缠绕数签名和临界耦合参数来识别新的拓扑子类。

Result: 识别出内部分支子类$\widetilde{W}^{0-}$（全局拓扑荷W=0，缠绕数签名$[-,+]$），与标准$W^{0-}$（通过嵌入$(+,-)$缠绕对实现拓扑延拓）不同，该子类通过在基础解序列末端附加额外稳定分支而形成。参数空间划分：Type I（$m_0\ge\hat{m}_{01}$）和Type II（$\hat{m}_{02}<m_0<\hat{m}_{01}$）属于$\widetilde{W}^{0-}$；$0<m_0\le\hat{m}_{02}$属于常规$W^{0-}$。

Conclusion: 数值结果验证了热力学拓扑形式学在无奇点正则黑洞中的自洽性，细化了针对非线性电动力学正则黑洞的拓扑判别标准，为后续更高维旋转正则黑洞的拓扑研究提供了理论参考。

Abstract: Based on the $φ$-mapping topological current theory, we systematically investigate the thermodynamic topology of regular Bardeen-AdS-class black holes coupled to nonlinear electrodynamics, a class of singularity-free geometries whose topological classification remains underexplored. We implement numerical calculations at two typical AdS radii $L=1$ and $L=15$. Within the fundamental $W^{0-}$ topological family, we identify a novel inner secondary subclass $\widetilde{W}^{0-}$. This result only refines and enriches the internal branch structure of the $W^{0-}$ family under the established classification scheme of five topological classes and four topological subclasses for black hole thermodynamics. This new subclass has a vanishing global topological charge $W=0$ with an inner-outer horizon winding-number signature $[-,+]$. Unlike standard $W^{0-}$ solutions that realize topological extension via embedded $(+,-)$ winding pairs, $\widetilde{W}^{0-}$ arises from attaching an extra stable branch to the end of the base solution sequence. Two critical coupling parameters $\hat{m}_{01}$ and $\hat{m}_{02}$ divide the full parameter space into distinct topological phases: Type I black holes with $m_0\ge\hat{m}_{01}$ and Type II solutions with $\hat{m}_{02}<m_0<\hat{m}_{01}$ belong to $\widetilde{W}^{0-}$, while the parameter region $0<m_0\le\hat{m}_{02}$ corresponds to the conventional $W^{0-}$ topology. Our numerical results verify the self-consistency of the thermodynamic topological formalism when applied to singularity-free regular black holes, refine the topological discrimination criteria tailored to nonlinear-electrodynamics regular black holes, and provide theoretical references for subsequent topological investigations of higher-dimensional rotating regular black holes under the same formalism.

</details>


### [153] [Coupled by Design: Computing Kerr-Newman Quasinormal Modes with a Hybrid SpectralPINN Solver](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14216)
*Alexandre M. Pombo*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文将\texttt{SpectralPINN}求解器扩展至Kerr-Newman黑洞的准正规模式计算，成功求解了两个耦合主偏微分方程组，生成了包含光子球模和近地平模的公开数据集，首次系统表征了矢量主导的光子球分支，并利用该数据集观察到特征值排斥现象及对爱因斯坦望远镜约束的预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将\texttt{SpectralPINN}求解器从可分离的单一方程（无电荷Kerr极限）推广至真正耦合的两场问题（Kerr-Newman），以求解引力场与电磁场耦合产生的两类解（光子球模与近地平模），并填补矢量主导光子球分支的系统性计算空白。

Method: 采用\texttt{SpectralPINN}（谱域物理信息神经网络）求解Kerr-Newman黑洞的两个耦合主偏微分方程；通过数值基准测试验证精度；计算并公开了多个模式的数据集。

Result: 相对频率误差最差约$10^{-4}$，大多数情况约$10^{-7}$；计算了五个光子球模（引力主导和矢量主导，最高$\ell=4$）及两个基态引力主导近地平模；首次系统地表征了矢量主导的光子球分支；观察到特征值排斥现象；预测了爱因斯坦望远镜对黑洞电荷质量比的约束。

Conclusion: \texttt{SpectralPINN}有效求解了耦合两场准正规模式问题，补充了近地平模和矢量主导模的计算空白，特征值排斥现象的观测及对爱因斯坦望远镜约束的预测显示了该数据集在天体物理观测中的潜在应用价值。

Abstract: We extend our \texttt{SpectralPINN} solver to the computation of Kerr-Newman quasinormal modes by applying it to solve the system of two coupled master PDEs -- advancing from the single, separable equation of the uncharged Kerr limit to a genuinely coupled two-field problem. The coupling between the gravitational and electromagnetic fields gives rise to two families of solutions: the photon-sphere, connecting with Kerr; and near-horizon, disconnected from the Kerr limit, each with two branches of solutions depending on the leading field: the gravitational- and vector-led. Benchmarking against publicly available datasets shows relative frequency errors of $\sim 10^{-4}$ worst case and $\sim10^{-7}$ for most cases. The computed public dataset spans five photon-sphere modes, both gravitational- and vector-led, up to $\ell=4$, as well as two fundamental gravitational-led near-horizon modes. The vector-led photon-sphere branch is computed and systematically characterized for the first time. We apply the dataset and observe the onset of the eigenvalue repulsion reported by Dias \textit{et al.}, to exclude an inter-polarization repulsion within the resolved domain, and to forecast Einstein Telescope constraints on the black-hole charge-to-mass ratio.

</details>


### [154] [Acceleration radiation and HBAR thermodynamics for atoms falling into a BTZ black hole: A CQM quantum-optics approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14281)
*H. E. Camblong,A. Chakraborty,C. R. Ordóñez,M. O. Scully,G. Valdivia-Mera,H. Wang*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了自由落入BTZ黑洞的原子在Boulware-like真空中辐射出具有Hawking温度$T_H$的普朗克光谱的辐射，揭示了这种热辐射与相对等效原理相关，并发现尽管(2+1)维引力的拓扑性质，BTZ黑洞的热力学和辐射性质仍由近 horizon 共形量子力学普遍描述，且光子辐射场的HBAR熵$S_{\mathcal P}$模仿了Bekenstein-Hawking熵$S_{\mathrm{BH}}=A/4$，包含正确的1/4比例因子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究自由落入BTZ黑洞的原子在Boulware-like真空中的辐射行为，验证其是否产生热Hawking辐射，并检验相对等效原理在其中的作用，同时研究(2+1)维BTZ黑洞的辐射与热力学性质是否仍受近 horizon 共形量子力学普遍支配。

Method: 采用量子光学方法，将原子解释为探测器，分析自由落入BTZ黑洞的原子与真空场模式的相互作用，描述原子辐射的量子过程。

Result: 自由落入BTZ黑洞的原子在Boulware-like真空中辐射出普朗克光谱，温度为Hawking温度$T_H$；随机初始时间的原子云产生热Hawking-like辐射；该辐射与相对等效原理相关；BTZ黑洞的辐射和热力学性质由近 horizon 共形量子力学普遍描述；光子辐射场的HBAR熵$S_{\mathcal P}$与Bekenstein-Hawking熵$S_{\mathrm{BH}}=A/4$一致。

Conclusion: 即使是在(2+1)维拓扑引力的BTZ黑洞背景下，自由落体原子仍可产生热Hawking辐射，且其热力学性质由普适的近 horizon 共形量子力学描述，光子辐射熵恰好复现了Bekenstein-Hawking熵的1/4面积律。

Abstract: Atoms falling freely into a Bañados-Teitelboim-Zanelli (BTZ) black hole in a Boulware-like vacuum are shown to emit radiation with a Planck spectrum at the Hawking temperature $T_{H}$. This leads to thermal Hawking-like radiation for a cloud of falling atoms prepared with random initial times. Moreover, the radiation is related to the relative equivalence principle, with the vacuum field modes accelerated with respect to the falling atom. The physics of the atom-field interactions is most easily described within a quantum optics approach, where each atom can be interpreted as a detector. Despite the topological nature of gravity in $(2+1)$ dimensions, the thermodynamic and radiation properties of BTZ black holes are still universally governed by the same near-horizon conformal quantum mechanics (CQM) applicable to higher-dimensional gravity. This universal conformal behavior is exhibited by all fields in the background of generic black holes, and generates an HBAR entropy $S_{\mathcal P}$ associated with the photon radiation field that mimics the Bekenstein-Hawking entropy $S_{\mathrm{BH}}=A/4$, proportional to the black-hole horizon area, and with the correct $1/4$ proportionality factor.

</details>


### [155] [Upwind embedded boundary SBP operators: New high order numerical schemes for arbitrarily shaped domains with Cartesian grids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14403)
*Conner Dailey*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在嵌入边界求和分部（SBP）框架下推导了新的高阶精度算子，通过引入迎风格式改善色散关系保持特性，并最小化边界误差。在带有球形切除边界的3D多块网格上对曲线标量波动方程的测试验证了算子的鲁棒性和准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有嵌入边界SBP方法虽然能处理非线性问题（如黑洞切除模拟），但边界误差较大且谱特性有限，亟需提高内部和边界闭包精度、优化色散特性以更好地处理波动方程。

Method: 基于嵌入边界SBP框架，推导新的高阶精度插值/外插算子以匹配边界闭包精度；引入广义迎风格式，改善色散关系保持特性；在中心块嵌入切除球面的3D多块网格上，用曲线标量波动方程进行数值测试。

Result: 新算子实现了更高阶的内部和边界精度，边界误差最小化；迎风格式相比传统SBP在波动方程上具有更好的色散关系保持性质；3D多块网格测试验证了算子的鲁棒性和准确性。

Conclusion: 新提出的嵌入边界SBP算子有效提升了精度和谱特性，适用于复杂几何边界（如黑洞切除）的高阶数值模拟，具有广阔的非线性问题应用前景。

Abstract: Embedded boundary summation by parts (SBP) methods define finite differencing based derivative operators with the added feature that the boundary need not coincide with a grid cell, allowing a boundary to be embedded on a regular Cartesian grid. This is achieved by the introduction of interpolation/extrapolation operators that match the accuracy of the boundary closure. These methods have been used to perform black hole excision simulations on a domain with a spherical boundary embedded in a regular Cartesian grid, demonstrating their usefulness for nonlinear problems. In this work, new operators are derived using this embedded boundary framework to increase the order of accuracy of the interior and boundary closure while minimizing the boundary error. Additionally, these novel operators improve the spectral properties on the grid by generalizing to an upwind scheme that has better dispersion relation preserving properties compared to traditional SBP schemes for wave equations. These operators are tested with the curvilinear scalar wave equation on a 3D multiblock grid with an excision sphere embedded in the center block to demonstrate the robustness and accuracy of these novel embedded operators.

</details>


### [156] [Scalarization of Charged Black Hole in Gauss-Bonnet Extended Starobinsky-Maxwell Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14620)
*Rui-Xi Zhu,Hai-Shan Liu*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文重新审视了Starobinsky引力中由Gauss-Bonnet不变量驱动的黑洞自发标量化现象。发现标量化解分裂为两个光滑分支，在共同的最小视界半径处相连，这种多分支结构在静态球对称标量化中此前未被报道。引入Maxwell场后，该结构依旧存在，且在特定参数范围内出现新的不连续分支。研究分析了从单一光滑分支到两个不连续分支的转变，并验证了所有带毛解精确满足第一定律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重新审视Starobinsky引力中Gauss-Bonnet不变量引发的黑洞自发标量化，探索是否存在之前未被发现的解结构特征，尤其是在引入电磁场后的行为变化。

Method: 在Starobinsky引力框架下补充Gauss-Bonnet不变量的理论，通过数值和解析方法分析静态球对称黑洞标量化解的分支结构；进一步引入Maxwell场考察带电情况下的分支演化；利用Maxwell热力学关系验证第一定律。

Result: 发现标量化解存在此前未被报道的多分支结构：两个光滑分支在共同最小视界半径处连接；引入Maxwell场后该结构保留，并在特定参数区间出现额外的不连续分支；所有带电带毛解均精确满足第一定律。

Conclusion: Starobinsky引力中Gauss-Bonnet驱动的自发标量化具有丰富的多分支结构，且带电情形下解空间更为复杂，但热力学一致性得到保证。

Abstract: We re-examine spontaneous scalarization of black holes within Starobinsky gravity supplemented by the Gauss-Bonnet invariant. A novel feature is uncovered: scalarized solutions split into two smooth branches that connect at a common minimal horizon radius, a multi-branch structure previously unreported for static, spherically symmetric scalarization. Extending the theory with a Maxwell field, we find that this multi-branch structure persists, while a new additional disconnected branch emerges within certain parameter ranges. We thoroughly analyse the transition from a single smooth branch to two disconnected branches. Lastly, we verify, employing the standard Maxwell thermodynamic relations, that all charged hairy solutions obey the first law exactly.

</details>


### [157] [Random walks around black holes and low-frequency X-ray variability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14626)
*Arthur G. Suvorov*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了强引力场中湍流流体中颗粒的随机动力学，将其建模为黎曼流形上的随机游走。通过求解黑洞背景下的福克-普朗克方程，发现颗粒逃逸概率高于平直空间，原因是事件视界附近径向元的拉伸提供了更大的漫游体积。模拟大量颗粒轨迹后，发现低频频谱指数较平直空间更平缓（因时间膨胀），高频更陡峭。施瓦西加权谱与天鹅座X-1硬态的低频X射线变率观测吻合，表明几何漂移对描述随机吸积过程可能重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为强引力场中湍流流体颗粒的随机动力学提供理论框架，解释致密天体吸积过程的内在变率，尤其是黑洞吸积流的随机行为。

Method: 将颗粒随机行走公式化为黎曼流形上的曲率加权游走，在施瓦西黑洞背景上求解福克-普朗克方程，并基于薄盘结构方程初始分布模拟大量颗粒轨迹。

Result: 黑洞背景下颗粒逃逸概率高于平直空间；低频频谱指数更平缓（时间膨胀导致），高频更陡峭；施瓦西加权谱与Cygnus X-1硬态观测匹配。

Conclusion: 几何漂移（曲率效应）在描述随机吸积过程中可能起重要作用，为理解黑洞吸积流的X射线变率提供了新的物理机制。

Abstract: The stochastic dynamics of a grain embedded within a turbulent fluid subject to strong gravitational fields can be formulated as a random walk on a Riemannian manifold. Such curvature-weighted walks provide a framework to model the intrinsic variability of accretion onto compact objects. By solving the relevant Fokker-Planck equation on a black hole background, we find the counterintuitive result that the escape probability of a grain is actually higher compared to flat space. This is a consequence of the stretching of radial cells near the event horizon: there is a greater spatial volume for the particle to wander through before being captured. By simulating a large number of grain trajectories, initially distributed on concentric shells with a density profile set by the thin-disc structure equations, we also study particle fluxes through the horizon. Shallower spectral indices emerge at low frequencies relative to flat space, primarily due to time dilation, and steeper ones at high frequencies. We find that Schwarzschild-weighted spectra broadly match observations of low-frequency X-ray variability from systems like Cygnus X-1 in their hard state, suggesting that geometric drifts may be important in describing stochastic accretion processes.

</details>


### [158] [Warped Spacelike Singularities and the $C^0$-Inextendibility of Birmingham-Kottler Spacetimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14741)
*Bobby EkaGunara*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文针对一类翘曲类空奇点，建立了连续洛伦兹延拓的局部障碍，该障碍通过两个可积性条件、相对翘曲因子的单调性条件以及纵向因子的发散性来表述。通过径向压缩终末因果迹得到边界图上的紧致时序分隔器，并结合纵向平移导致的内在发散与延拓图中的均匀控制，证明了一类Birmingham-Kottler时空的C⁰不可延拓性。


<details>
